# CompilerSutra Weekly — #001, November 7 2026

**What changed in compilers this week.**

**Issue:** #001  
**Published:** 2026-11-07 (Saturday)  
**Reporting period:** 2026-10-30 → 2026-11-06 (UTC, Fri→Fri, end exclusive)

Welcome to issue **#001** of CompilerSutra Weekly — a Saturday digest of compiler engineering across LLVM, GCC, MLIR, AI compilers, GPU compilers, and related toolchains. This week covers **2026-10-30 → 2026-11-06** (UTC). Activity observed in: gcc, llvm, mlir. Entries are selected and grouped from repository activity; every link goes to the original source. Descriptions stay factual — we do not invent performance claims or completion status.

## Contents

- [LLVM](#llvm)
- [GCC](#gcc)
- [MLIR](#mlir)
- [Complete change log](#complete-change-log)
